# Skills · C · part 258

864 entries, cpu-optimization to craft-article-opening. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **cpu-optimization**: Reducing CPU cost once profiling has shown the frame is CPU-bound - algorithmic complexity, per-frame work, allocation and garbage pressure, cache behaviour, and threading. Use after performance-profiling-method has identified a CPU bottleneck, when frame time is dominated by game logic or script execution, or when garbage collection causes periodic hitches. ([ibrohim1234567881717/game-dev-ai-skills](https://github.com/ibrohim1234567881717/game-dev-ai-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/core/performance/cpu-optimization))

- **cpu-optimization-playbook**: Master index + decision flow for optimizing an SGLang model op on Intel Xeon CPUs. Use this first; it decides WHICH technique skills to load and in WHAT order. ([bharat-kaul/sglang-cpu-opt](https://github.com/bharat-kaul/sglang-cpu-opt/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/kernel-optimization/cpu-optimization-playbook))
